  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

/* Desktop product links are visible in the header; keep the mobile selector. */
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
    .nav-dropdown-products-selector-trigger {
        display: none !important;
    }
}

/* Product tabs sit beside the logo, above each product's own navigation. */
@media (min-width: 1024px) {
    #navbar .h-full.relative.flex-1.flex.items-center.gap-x-4.min-w-0 {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: auto auto minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    }
    #navbar .h-full.relative.flex-1.flex.items-center.gap-x-4.min-w-0 > div:first-child,
    #navbar .h-full.relative.flex-1.flex.items-center.gap-x-4.min-w-0 > div:nth-child(3) {
        display: contents;
    }
    #navbar nav[aria-label="Main"] {
        grid-column: 2;
        grid-row: 1;
        margin: 0;
    }
    #navbar .h-full.relative.flex-1.flex.items-center.gap-x-4.min-w-0 > div:nth-child(2) {
        grid-column: 3;
        grid-row: 1;
    }
    #navbar div:has(> [data-component-name="theme-preference-menu"]) {
        grid-column: 4;
        grid-row: 1;
    }
    #navbar .navbar-link a:is([href="/transform"], [href="/pipelines"], [href="/open-source/introduction/overview"]) {
        padding: 8px 14px;
        border-radius: 8px;
        min-height: 40px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: #475569;
        background: transparent;
        text-decoration: none;
    }
    .dark #navbar .navbar-link a:is([href="/transform"], [href="/pipelines"], [href="/open-source/introduction/overview"]) { color: #cbd5e1; }
    #navbar .navbar-link a:is([href="/transform"], [href="/pipelines"], [href="/open-source/introduction/overview"]):hover { background: #e2e8f0; }
    .dark #navbar .navbar-link a:is([href="/transform"], [href="/pipelines"], [href="/open-source/introduction/overview"]):hover { background: #243239; }
    #navbar:has([data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"] [title="Transform"]) .navbar-link a[href="/transform"],
    #navbar:has([data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"] [title="Pipelines"]) .navbar-link a[href="/pipelines"],
    #navbar:has([data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"] [title="Open Source"]) .navbar-link a[href="/open-source/introduction/overview"] {
        background: #cffafe;
        color: #155e75;
    }
    .dark #navbar:has([data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"] [title="Transform"]) .navbar-link a[href="/transform"],
    .dark #navbar:has([data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"] [title="Pipelines"]) .navbar-link a[href="/pipelines"],
    .dark #navbar:has([data-component-part="products-selector-trigger"] [title="Open Source"]) .navbar-link a[href="/open-source/introduction/overview"] {
        background: #123840;
        color: #22d3ee;
    }
    #navbar .navbar-link a:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #06b6d4; outline-offset: 3px; }
}

class="mdx-content @container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Parse your first document" data-page-href="/transform/first-request" id="content"><span data-as="p">Transform turns documents into parsed content and structured data. Use the web app walkthrough below, or skip to <a href="#make-your-first-api-request" class="link">your first API request</a> to start with code. You do not need to complete both paths.</span>
<div role="list" class="steps ml-3.5 mt-10 mb-6"><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">1</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Log in</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p"><a href="https://transform.unstructured.io" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Log in to Transform</a>. If you do not have access, contact your account team.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">2</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Upload a document</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Upload your own document or download the <a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Unstructured-IO/docs-samples/main/pdf/ACME_Corp_Financial_Report.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">ACME Corp Annual Report sample PDF</a> to try the flow. Check the <a class="link" href="/transform/limits">supported file types</a> if you are unsure whether your file is supported.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">3</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Parse the document</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Choose <strong>Parse</strong>. When processing finishes, review the parsed content alongside your document.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">4</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Extract structured data (optional)</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Choose <strong>Extract</strong> if you need specific fields as structured data. Define the fields you need, then review the extracted values.</span></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-gray-200 dark:from-white/10 via-80% to-transparent [.step-container:has([data-component-part=step-content]:empty)_&amp;]:hidden" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">5</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Use Get Code</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Open <strong>Get Code</strong> to carry the flow into an API integration. Use your API key when running the code in your application.
See <a class="link" href="/transform/get-code">integrate with Get Code</a> for the next steps.</span></div></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="review-the-output"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#review-the-output" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Review the output</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Compare the parsed content and any extracted values with your source document. See <a class="link" href="/transform/results">result interpretation</a> for details.</span>
<span data-as="p">See <a class="link" href="/transform/recovery">recovery guidance</a> if an upload or processing step fails.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="make-your-first-api-request"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#make-your-first-api-request" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Make your first API request</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">You can Parse a document directly through the API without uploading it in the web app first.</span>
<span data-as="p">The <code>-i</code> option prints response headers so you can retain <code>Location</code> if processing continues. Use your API key in <code>UNSTRUCTURED_API_KEY</code> and a supported document saved as <code>document.pdf</code>. No workspace parameter is required.</span>
<span data-as="p">To try the example without your own file, download the <a href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Unstructured-IO/docs-samples/main/pdf/ACME_Corp_Financial_Report.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">ACME Corp Annual Report sample PDF</a> and save it as <code>document.pdf</code> in your project directory.</span>

<span data-as="p">A synchronous request keeps the connection open until parsing finishes. Multi-page documents can take about a minute or longer. That wait is expected. For an immediate response, see <a class="link" href="/transform/jobs#request-asynchronous-processing">asynchronous processing</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">HTTP 200 returns a Parse result directly. This response was captured from the ACME sample PDF on September 12, 2026. The Markdown is shortened and the result ID is replaced with an example UUID:</span>

<span data-as="p">Read the parsed text in <code>markdown</code>. Save <code>id</code> to Extract from the same Parse later. Check <code>status</code> and <code>warnings</code> before using the output. <code>metadata</code> describes the document, <code>format_version</code> identifies the result format, and <code>source</code> identifies the input file and its expiry. The source values in this shortened example are representative.</span>
<span data-as="p">This Markdown example has empty <code>elements</code> and <code>extracted_data</code> arrays. General purpose: this script provides download capability for files that\n// won't reliably save using a plain anchor tag's download attribute alone.\n// For example, GitHub's raw-content host displays the contents of a .py file\n// in the browser instead of downloading it, which isn't the desired behavior\n// for a sample script meant to be downloaded and run locally.\n//\n// Fetches the file and forces a save dialog client-side; a plain \u003ca download\u003e tag\n// doesn't reliably work here because\n// (a) GitHub's raw-content host is\n// cross-origin (not on the same site as the documentation), and\n// (b) doesn't send a Content-Disposition header\n// (which could instruct the browser to save the file, overriding everything else).\n//\n// Because of (a) and (b), the browser ignores the download attribute and falls back\n// to its own default handling based on content-type. For a .py file, GitHub sends\n// content-type: text/plain, so the browser defaults to displaying the file rather\n// than downloading it.\n//\n// PDF files meet (a) and (b) as well, but GitHub sends back\n// content-type: application/octet-stream, which basically means \"this is just some\n// unspecified binary data\". Since the browser has no built-in way to render that,\n// its default is to download the file instead of displaying it.\n//\n// To work around this, downloadScript() below builds its own local link whose scheme\n// is blob: rather than https:. That makes it page-owned rather than cross-origin,\n// so (a) no longer applies, and the browser always honors the download attribute\n// regardless of content-type.\n//\n// To wire up a new download link, add these two attributes to a plain\n// \u003ca href=\"...\"\u003e tag:\n//   data-download-script                   marks the link for this script to catch\n//   data-download-filename=\"whatever.ext\"   the name the saved file should get\n//\n// Usage example:\n//   \u003ca href=\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Unstructured-IO/docs-samples/main/transform/sample-code/extract-quickstart.py\" data-download-script data-download-filename=\"extract-quickstart.py\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eextract-quickstart.py\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n//\n// This example can be found in transform/quickstart-python.mdx.\n\nasync function downloadScript(url, filename) {\n  try {\n    // Fetch the file's bytes ourselves, instead of letting the browser navigate to it.\n    const response = await fetch(url);\n\n    // Wrap the bytes in a Blob: page-owned data, no longer a cross-origin request.\n    const blob = await response.blob();\n\n    // Create a local blob: URL for that Blob.\n    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n\n    // Click a hidden link pointing at that local blob: URL with download set;\n    // browsers always honor this since blob: URLs aren't cross-origin.\n    const link = document.createElement(\"a\");\n    link.href = blobUrl;\n    link.download = filename;\n\n    // The link we just built looks like this in the executing code:\n    // \u003ca href=\"blob:https://docs.unstructured.io/1a2b3c4d-5e6f-7g8h-9i0j\"\n    //    download=\"extract_quickstart.py\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\n    // This link.click() simulates an actual mouse click on the anchor URL we just built,\n    // triggering the browser to evaluate the link and decide to download it, since\n    // it points to a local blob: URL rather than a cross-origin address.\n    link.click();\n\n    // Release the blob: URL now that the download has started; otherwise the\n    // browser keeps its data in memory for as long as the page stays open.\n    URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);\n  } catch {\n    window.location.href = url;\n  }\n}\n\n// Event delegation on document, rather than attaching listeners to each link\n// directly, so this keeps working after client-side page navigation swaps in\n// new [data-download-script] links without this file re-running.\ndocument.addEventListener(\"click\", function (e) {\n  const link = e.target.closest(\"[data-download-script]\");\n  if (!link) return;\n  e.preventDefault();\n  downloadScript(link.href, link.dataset.downloadFilename);\n});"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"4c:[\"$\",\"$L56\",null,{\"customJsDisabled\":false,\"jsFiles\":[{\"_id\":\"6633c08af6b689bb0734ced3\",\"filePath\":\"script.js\",\"subdomain\":\"unstructured-53\",\"__v\":0,\"content\":\"var
